# Seed 58 on the AMD Ryzen 7 PRO 8840HS - the anchoring and constraint test: from scratch and free to choose the encoding, it **passed every carried run at the small end (-10.5% bytes), at 32 bits (+21.5%) and in hypervolume (+11.1%)** - plain CV8 - and came within 3.4% at the 64-bit fast end - tag-2; not small at either width (Iteration 384)

`seed 58 12h then experiment compare-fronts.py --rounds 6 --widths 8,4 --frontier --cpus 2,8`, **nocarry**
(the hand-made designs and founders, no earlier front) and **without the `--require tag2`** every run since
Iteration 71 has had - the anchoring and constraint test (HANDOFF item 4c; the owner, Iteration 374), selected
at both widths. Commit c49de1703 (Iteration 378): the after-seed-57 batch applied (non-PIE 32-bit engines,
superinstruction pairs named right). The chain 03:12 - 14:39 UTC Oct 18; **the remote checks' copying held the
evolution back 55 minutes** (started 03:17:26, the run 04:12:39 - the old path; Iteration 379's fix is from
seed 59). 34,174 designs, generations 0-133: 24,831 alive, 9,149 screened (27.7% from generation 5), 183
refused before running (the reach limit at scale 0); 8 died on the kernel workload, 3 did not compile - **2 of
a new kind**: the label `L_t2call4` used but not defined in vm-opcall-bodies.h - both tag-2 designs with opcall
`>R`, made by a mutation to bytehdr=0, a gene the tag-2 requirement had held on (open item 0d).

**The verdict.** At 64 bits, against all the other runs in one session (a positive figure is this run ahead):
fastest **-3.4%** (52917e2595, 0.131), smallest **+10.51%** (09de03f091, **14,146 bytes against the others'
15,633**), memory **+4 KB** (116 KB against 120), hypervolume **+11.1%**, best Q **-5.3%** - **not small**, by
its gains at the small end and in hypervolume. At 32 bits its fastest is 0.310 (**d55f05136f, plain CV8**),
**+21.5%** against every other run's best in the same session (seed 57's database: 0.377) - **not small at both
widths**. Its 64-bit fastest there, 52917e2595, is 0.379. The Q-best of all runs stays seed 57's e6b5f1749e,
0.151 at 27,944 bytes, 160 KB - Q 0.204 of s6's; this run's in-run best Q cd386f487e, Q 0.207.

**The constraint test answered - the requirement was costing the small end and the 32-bit end.** Free to
choose, the search tried more plain CV8 than tag-2 (18,153 designs to 16,021) and kept both, by objective:
of the front's 171, **the small third (14,146-16,793 bytes) 57 of 57 plain CV8, the middle 36 plain and 21
tag-2, the fast third (26,136-36,213 bytes) 53 of 57 tag-2**; the fastest at 64 bits tag-2 (1b5ac698ce), the
fastest at 32 bits and the smallest plain (d55f05136f, 09de03f091). The JIT exactly on the tag-2 designs (74).
So tag-2 is chosen on merit where speed at 64 bits counts - and the plain-CV8 territory it gains, smaller
than any carried design and the fastest at 32 bits (tag-2 costs ~36% at 32 bits, results/drafts/skew-32.md),
is territory no carried run could enter.

**The anchoring question is not separated by this run.** Its gains are plain-CV8 designs, which the
requirement alone kept from the carried runs; whether starting fresh also contributed, it cannot say. What it
shows: from scratch, 12 hours reach within 3.4% of the carried fast end (seed 36, 2 h 40 min: 12% behind), and
the **structure** is the carried runs' - every always-on gene the same (ops10, spec, fsys, bss, lean, thinhdr,
bytehdr, varcall, the encoding machinery at 100% in both; msc, tail, scale, jitos off in both) - with the
**tuning** different: gene-usage.py against seeds 53-57, beyond the 17-point scale of two carried runs, dskew
-30, opcall -28, rtiplus +23, d256 +22, regpin -21, bfind -21, opt +20, lto -19, abortif +19. The follow-up
that separates the two (HANDOFF 4c): the anchoring test as first specified at Iteration 373 - nocarry *with*
tag2 - is seed 59.

**Superinstructions (HANDOFF item 1), first half answered:** with the pool named right, 73 of the front's 74
tag-2 designs build pairs (the report lists only what express() builds) - before, the gene was inert in ~97%
of fronts (Iteration 367). Whether the pairs buy anything is the second half: a measurement, pairs knocked out.

**The facts** (seed-facts.py): the front 171 designs (mutation 132, borrowed 21, crossover 18; via the attic
10), all 171 with a 32-bit speed; speed against 32-bit speed over the front, Spearman 0.573 - the two
objectives well apart. Children: mutation 54.3%, crossover 33.9%, borrowed 11.8%; via the attic 5,431. tos on
110; kfast 151; tfind 102; opbody 139; regpin 16 (12 of them 3); bfind 25; clang (compiler) 104; jitimg 9;
jitvar 8. At 0 on every design of the front: kstack, sysc, tailpn, alignhot, nogcse (and msc, tail, jitos).
Format-10 words: ?BRANCH8 171, (LOOP) 171, I 171, (+LOOP) 170, UNLOOP 169, BRANCH8 167, DUP?BRANCH 154,
THREAD-FIND 146, CMOVE 143, SWAP+I 132.

**Held out** (heldout.py): sieve over the 171, Spearman 0.883 with the selection speed (seeds 53-57: 0.959,
0.953, 0.959, 0.963, 0.946) - lower, as a front spanning two encodings and the whole size range would rank;
the selection's most flattered are plain-CV8 designs (9c71f11675, c5d84029a4, 7e0996063e: 3.61x slower held
out than selected).

**Predictions: 4 of 5 as written** (results/predictions.md, the first scored since seed 55's): 1 met (3.4%
behind), 2 missed (tag-2 on 43% of the front, not 60%), 3 met (plain CV8 57%, the 32-bit fastest plain), 4 met
as written (0.310 against seed 57's recorded 0.337) though not like for like (21.5% ahead of its 0.377 in the
same session), 5 met (no rejected design faster on the Tegra than the front's fastest).

**The checks - all four gathered** (results/xcheck-seed58.md), of seed 57's front and, first, designs seed
57's selection rejected (xcheck --rejected, item 4d): the Tegra (ARMv7) Spearman **0.973** (13 measured; 3 did
not link there - two of them front designs with regpin=1 and lto=1, xcheck keeping only ld's last line);
dream 64-bit **0.959**, 32-bit **0.875** (the width's skew), the default build 0.960. **No rejected design
faster on any host than the front's fastest** (Tegra 3 measured / 0, dream-32 6 / 0, dream-64 6 / 0).

---
# Evolved VM designs

25025 designs evaluated, 24831 alive, 9149 screened - not measured (Iteration 200). Speed is the geometric mean of median cpu time / hand-made s6, long workloads (Iteration 100) over kernel, fib, corpus, loop, bubble, ackermann,
relative to s6-cv8b; size is the self-hosting image, binary the stripped engine. sieve, parse is held out.
The front is over three objectives, equal (Iteration 75): speed, binary + image, peak memory - memory within 8 KB a tie (Iteration 77).


The Q-best design (binary + image x run time x memory - the knee; information): cd386f487e, speed 0.136, binary + image 30,453, memory 164 KB - Q 0.207 of s6-cv8b's.
